- **wgcna-co-expression-module-detection**: Use when input is a processed single-cell expression matrix with cell-level metadata and the goal is to infer co-expression modules and identify which modules track a trait such as patient survival status. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/wgcna-co-expression-module-detection--6061abef195e5222))
- **wgcna-co-expression-module-detection**: Use when you have an RNA-seq table of counts plus sample phenotype metadata (e.g., mutant/wild-type Lafora disease samples) and want to find co-expressed gene groups (CEGs) that correlate with phenotypic data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/wgcna-co-expression-module-detection--0b3b74c66789bebb))
- **wgcna-coexpression-network-inference**: Use when the input is a preprocessed single-cell expression matrix (e.g. .h5ad/HDF5/loom) with cell metadata and the goal is to infer gene co-expression modules and hubs for specific cell types or conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/wgcna-coexpression-network-inference))
- **wgcna-comparative-module-inference**: Use when a co-expression/co-abundance matrix (e.g. genus-level counts from QIIME 2) must be partitioned into modules by both SGCRNA and WGCNA to compare module granularity and preprocessing retention. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/wgcna-comparative-module-inference))
- **wgcna-gene-module-detection**: Use when a cell-type-specific co-expression matrix (e.g., from CS-CORE or diff_CSCORE) needs to be partitioned into co-expressed gene modules for downstream GO enrichment of disease-relevant pathways. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/wgcna-gene-module-detection))
- **wgcna-module-detection**: Use when you have a metabolite-by-sample quantification matrix (e.g. LC-MS peak areas from a pseudotargeted metabolomics run) and need to group co-accumulating metabolites into modules for biomarker metabolite screening. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/wgcna-module-detection))
- **wgcna-module-detection**: Use when you have a bulk RNA-seq count matrix across many samples (e.g. 38 female hippocampus 3xTg-AD/WT samples) and need to find co-expressed gene modules and correlate them with external traits. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/wgcna-module-detection))
- **wgcna-module-detection-metabolomics**: Use when a normalized metabolite measurement table (e.g. Metabolon®-style data with SUB_PATHWAY annotations) should be partitioned into co-abundance modules and tested against sample traits such as tumor vs normal status or gender, without predefined metabolite sets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/wgcna-module-detection-metabolomics))
- **wgcna-module-eigenmetabolite-screening**: Use when analyzing a pseudotargeted metabolomics feature/peak-area table (metabolites × samples) across a mutant or treatment panel to find co-abundance modules and rank samples by module eigenmetabolite for biomarker metabolite screening. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/wgcna-module-eigenmetabolite-screening))
- **wgcna-module-richness-characterization**: Use when WGCNA subnetworks (e.g. seasonal 'grey' and 'blue' modules) from a Monterey Bay-style eDNA co-occurrence network must be characterized by taxon richness and correlated against environmental variables such as chlorophyll a or sea surface temperature. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/wgcna-module-richness-characterization))
- **wgcna-module-trait-association-testing**: Use when a PyWGCNA co-expression network has already been built and you need module–trait correlations (e.g., module eigengene vs. genotype or age/time point) plus GO/KEGG/REACTOME enrichment of the correlated module. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/wgcna-module-trait-association-testing))
- **wgcna-network-query**: 通过线上 Potato Agent WGCNA API 查询马铃薯 DMv8.2 基因的网络归属、模块、kME、TOM 排名共表达邻居、跨网络模块重叠和共享共表达边，并生成与 Interface WGCNA 查看器视觉语义一致的 SVG 或 PDF 矢量网络图。用于回答 leaf、stem、root、reproductive 或 tuberization 网络中的 WGCNA、共表达、候选 hub gene、模块成员、跨组织网络保守性和网络绘图问题。 ([biojiayuxin/potato-agent](https://github.com/biojiayuxin/potato-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/potato-knowledge-bioinformatics/wgcna-network-query) · ★1)
- **wgcna-power-threshold**: Given a dataset name, find its expression matrix on the VEuPathDB workflow server, rsync it locally, and determine the WGCNA soft-threshold power. ([VEuPathDB/dataset-cleaner](https://github.com/VEuPathDB/dataset-cleaner/tree/HEAD/skills/wgcna-power-threshold))
- **wgcna-scale-free-parameter-tuning**: Use when a co-expression analysis must assume a scale-free topology and needs the R WGCNA package run with manually tuned parameters (soft-thresholding power) on a gene-by-sample count matrix, e.g. as a baseline for comparison with scale-free-free methods like SGCRNA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/wgcna-scale-free-parameter-tuning))
- **wget**: GNU Wget download tool ([javimosch/supercli](https://github.com/javimosch/supercli/tree/HEAD/plugins/wget/skills/quickstart) · ★58)
- **wget-reader**: Fetch data from URLs. Use when asked to download content, fetch remote files, or read web data. ([beshkenadze/claude-skills-marketplace](https://github.com/beshkenadze/claude-skills-marketplace/tree/HEAD/skills/utility/wget-reader) · ★4)

- **wgf-ability-architect**: 게임의 전체 **캐릭터 능력/스킬 시스템**을 설계하고 게임에 입힌다 — 액티브·패시브·이동기(대시·더블점프·갈고리)· 궁극기·리액션(패링)·자원(마나/스태미나/기력)·쿨다운·충전·콤보/연계·캔슬·시너지·빌드·스킬트리/특성·진행/획득까지 "캐릭터가 *무엇을 할 수 있고, 그것이 어떻게 자라고 조합되는가*"를 담당하는 상위 스킬. **여기서 '스킬'은 게임 캐릭터의 능력을 뜻한다 — Claude Code 의 스킬(클로드 스킬)과 다르다.** 플랫포머의 더블점프 한 개부터 디아블로급 스킬트리까지 폭넓은 스펙트럼을 커버하되, **복잡도를 가장 먼저 가른다** — 디폴트는 코어 동사 위 능력 0~1개에서 한 칸씩 정당화한다. 현재 게임의 코어 동사·장르·STORY.md 서사·플랫폼을 먼저 분석하고, 의도가 모호하면 탑다운 1문1답으로 끈질기게 캐물어, 매 라운드 Claude가 먼저 참신한 능력·콤보·시너지 아이디어를 제안하며 사용자가 고르거나 비틀게 한다. 검증된 능력 설계 이론(자원·기회비용 / 선딜·발동·후딜 게임필 / enabler·payoff 시너지로 빌드 재미 / 콤보 연계 / 스킬트리·능력게이트 진행 / 파워예산·카운터플레이 밸런스 / 능력 판타지·정체… ([v0o0v/web-game-forge](https://github.com/v0o0v/web-game-forge/tree/HEAD/skills/wgf-ability-architect))
